Regression from #32865 (`d81fed2dbd`, merged 2026-09-28). It repointed two compatibility re-exports from `@elizaos/remote-control-host/*` to `@elizaos/plugin-browser/remote-control/*`:

- `packages/app/src/security/remote-control-crypto.ts` → `@elizaos/plugin-browser/remote-control/crypto`
- `packages/ui/src/api/direct-cloud-endpoints.ts` → `@elizaos/plugin-browser/remote-control/cloud-endpoints`

`packages/agent/scripts/build-mobile-bundle.ts` aliases `@elizaos/plugin-browser` **and every subpath** to `plugins/plugin-browser/src/mobile.ts` (longest-prefix match, `startsWith(`${name}/`)`). Both subpaths therefore resolved to the browser stub, which exports none of the crypto or cloud-endpoint symbols.

The mobile graph needs them. `packages/app/src/mobile-agent-entry.ts` registers `@elizaos/app/mobile-remote-target` (also `aospCore` in `packages/agent/src/runtime/core-plugins.ts:126`). That plugin imports the electrobun remote-target executor/rpc → `remote-target-runner.ts` → `remote-control-crypto`, and `packages/ui/src/api/client-cloud.ts` imports `direct-cloud-endpoints`.

No CI job runs `build:mobile` except the manual `android-build.yml`, which has no runs, so the break is invisible to develop CI.

Once `build:mobile` builds again, `build:android:cloud(:debug)` still fails its own Play audits on develop. These fail for the same reason: nothing runs this path in CI.

1. **Pre-gradle audit**: `capacitor.plugins.json packages differ from the Play allowlist`. `@elizaos/capacitor-network-policy`, added by `2e894ceebe` for on-device model downloads, is neither allowlisted nor stripped.
2. **Artifact audit** (`ANDROID_PLAY_MANIFEST_ALLOWLIST_FAILED`): the password-manager bridge added in `04c5080663` leaks into the Play manifest. That means `PasswordsActivity`, `BrowserNativeMessagingService` and its `org.chromium.chrome...NATIVE_MESSAGING` action, `USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT`, and `<queries>` packages `com.onepassword.android`, `com.x8bit.bitwarden` and `org.chromium.chrome`.

# Change

`build-mobile-bundle.ts` resolves the six platform-neutral remote-control protocol modules to source: `crypto`, `controller`, `runtime-store`, `secure-store-contract`, `cloud-client`, `cloud-endpoints`.

- Their imports are only `node:crypto`, `@elizaos/core`, `@elizaos/core/contracts/remote-control` and `@elizaos/plugin-elizacloud/cloud-config/domain-contract`. I checked each file's imports.
- The longer alias wins over the `@elizaos/plugin-browser` stub.
- `remote-control/browser-controller` stays stubbed: it imports `../browser-service` and `../targets/remote-device-target`.
- The plugin root and all other subpaths are unchanged.

**Android Play cloud policy** (`packages/app/scripts/mobile/android/cloud-policy.ts`, `strip.ts`):

- `@elizaos/capacitor-network-policy` joins `ANDROID_CLOUD_STRIPPED_NATIVE_PLUGINS`.
  - It serves on-device model downloads: only `ElizaAgentService` and `ElizaBionicInferenceServer` use it natively, and both are already stripped.
  - The JS side is a bare side-effect import in `native-plugin-entrypoints.ts`, next to other stripped plugins (camera, canvas, appblocker, bun-runtime).
- `PasswordsActivity` and `BrowserNativeMessagingService` join `ANDROID_CLOUD_STRIPPED_COMPONENTS`, and their Java sources join `ANDROID_CLOUD_STRIPPED_JAVA_FILES`.
  - `CredentialManagerPlugin.java` is stripped too. Its only behavior is starting `PasswordsActivity`, and nothing in the cloud tree registers it.
- `ANDROID_CLOUD_STRIPPED_QUERY_PACKAGES` (Chromium placeholder, Bitwarden, 1Password, healthdata) replaces the one-off healthdata `<queries>` regex in `strip.ts`.
- `ANDROID_CLOUD_MANIFEST_MERGER_REMOVED_QUERY_PACKAGES` adds a `tools:node="remove"` marker for `${elizaChromiumPackageName}`, because the kept `plugin-native-browser-surface` library re-adds it through manifest merge (same pattern as `ANDROID_CLOUD_MANIFEST_MERGER_REMOVED_PERMISSIONS`).
- **Bug fix:** `ANDROID_CLOUD_STRIPPED_PERMISSIONS` listed `"android.permission.USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT"`, while `removeAndroidPermissionRequests` prepends `android.permission.`. The entry never matched, so the permission always leaked. It is now `"USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT"`.
- The Play allowlists themselves are unchanged (the manifest `queryPackages` allowlist is still `[]`).
  - Everything above makes the cloud build meet the existing policy.
  - Allowing the password-manager surface in the Play client would be a separate product/policy decision.

For the mobile bundle fix, I looked at two alternatives:
- **Un-stubbing plugin-browser subpaths wholesale** would pull desktop-only modules into the phone bundle.
- **Aliasing only `crypto`** fixed the first 6 errors and exposed the same failure for `cloud-endpoints` (`DEFAULT_DIRECT_CLOUD_BASE_URL`, `stripTrailingSlashes`, … imported by `packages/ui/src/api/client-cloud.ts:79-84`). So the fix covers the protocol-module class.

6c:T80f,Adds `@unbrowse/plugin-unbrowse` to the community registry. The entry is `packages/registry/entries/third-party/unbrowse__plugin-unbrowse.json`, and `generated-registry.json` was regenerated with `bun run --cwd packages/registry generate`. The only change is 46 added lines for this entry.

**What it is.** An elizaOS plugin for [Unbrowse](https://unbrowse.ai). It turns websites into callable APIs through the hosted Unbrowse MCP.
- `WEB_FETCH` reads a page as clean markdown. The JS render falls back to plain HTTP.
- `UNBROWSE_DISCOVER` finds a learned API for a site.
- `UNBROWSE_RUN` runs a task on it.
- `UNBROWSE_RESUME` answers `input_required` on the same run.
- `UNBROWSE_BROWSE` opens the cloud browser, with saved logins filled from Unbrowse's vault.
- An `UNBROWSE` provider reports connection state and usage rules.

It is meant to replace `plugin-browser` for web reads and site tasks. The README documents the load order: action names are first-wins.

**Source.** [unbrowse-ai/unbrowse/plugins/elizaos](https://github.com/unbrowse-ai/unbrowse/tree/main/plugins/elizaos), MIT. `@elizaos/core` is a peer dependency, and there are no runtime dependencies because the SDK is bundled. The API key is read through `runtime.getSetting` and is never echoed; the tests assert that.

**Tested.**
- 46 offline tests, including a real `@elizaos/core` 1.7.2 `AgentRuntime` for action registration, first-wins behaviour and similes routing.
- Live tests against the hosted service: page read, a Hacker News top-stories run, and discover.
- Typecheck and build are clean. CI runs on every push in the source repo.

**Checklist**
- [x] `bun run --cwd packages/registry validate` passes (50 third-party entries).
- [x] Not in the reserved `@elizaos/*` scope.
- [x] Public repository, MIT.
- [x] `keywords` include `elizaos`.
- [ ] Published to npm: `@unbrowse/plugin-unbrowse@12.1.0` is going out from the source repo's release workflow and is not live yet. Please hold the merge until `npm view @unbrowse/plugin-unbrowse` resolves. I'll comment here when it does.6d:T5b7,Fixes the last red lane on develop: `canonical / Zero-Key scenario runner` fails on `convo.context-hygiene` with "evaluator advertised context restoration with nothing deferred". Seen on 729ce4151b (run 36377571213) and reproduced locally on 6a36364ee9.

**Why.** #32870 added this scenario when the evaluator template interpolated only the applicable decisions. #32835 then deliberately changed that design for prompt caching (`plugins/plugin-assistant/src/prompts/evaluator.ts`, `evaluatorContextRouteNames` doc comment):
- the reusable protocol text and response schema keep the full decision superset, so the prefix stays stable;
- eligibility moves into the per-call `# Current decision state`: `Available restoration routes: …` plus the restoration rule, only when applicable;
- `enforceEvaluatorDecisionState` rejects unavailable restoration after decoding.

The scenario still banned `RESTORE_*` anywhere in the evaluator text and schema enum, so it could never pass.

**Change.** The scenario now checks the decision state. With nothing deferred it must show `Available restoration routes: none` and carry no restoration guidance. The schema must still offer `FINISH`. No product code changes.

**Verified locally on develop 6a36364ee9:**
- `convo.context-hygiene` passes.
- Full `bun run --cwd packages/testing test:pr:e2e` passes: 27/27, 9/9, 14/14 and 34/34.
- biome check is clean.
